Amino-acid and peptide utilization genes — Prochlorococcus MED4 and Alteromonas macleodii HOT1A3

Spike draft (not reviewed). One row per (locus tag, role). Prochlorococcus MED4: 1,973 genes in genome, 141 in this table (146 rows) · Alteromonas macleodii HOT1A3: 4,028 genes in genome, 324 in this table (334 rows)

How to read: membership comes from ontology ids / KG routes (TCDB, GO, EC, MEROPS, KEGG KO, SBP metric), never from product text. Product text is used only to label rows (aa_class keywords, housekeeping / protein-translocation subroles). weak_evidence_only = "yes" marks uptake rows supported only by a TCDB family-walk, and peptidase rows supported only by a GO term. The shared_with_biosynthesis column is [interpretation] (rule-based, see notes per row). aa_class "peptides" is assigned to every peptidase.
